953 entries, video-editor to visual-debug-iterator. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **video-editor**: Edits recorded footage end to end — talking-head cleanup (silence, filler and retake cuts, audio denoise, loudness normalisation, captions retimed through the cuts), ad and social cutdowns, reframing, transcription and subtitle files — driven by an edit decision list and reported only at the state it actually reached, backed by measured output evidence. Also plans a self-recording (outline, script, capture command the user runs) and edits the result. Not for generated animation or motion graphics (motion-designer), a clickable prototype (prototyper), a still image (the image-generation skill)… ([AlexanderVegazo26/agents](https://github.com/AlexanderVegazo26/agents/blob/HEAD/sdlc-suite/agents/video-editor.md) · 4 copies)

- **video-editor**: Edits the raw take of a feature (from factory:ui-tester mode `demo`) into a short, branded product video with Hyperframes: the recording opened right on the change and cut of its waits, camera zooms and highlights on each step from its timeline and captions (intro and outro cards only when the hub's GUIDE asks), then renders mp4, gif and poster. Never records, never edits a repo. Give it the raw video, its events.json, the script, the feature's title and one-liner, the hub's changelogs/GUIDE.md (brand and video rules), the format and the out dir. Read-only apart from that one file. ([tailamphat/visor](https://github.com/tailamphat/visor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visor-analyst.md) · ★3)
- **visor-approver**: Approves or rejects a /visor-dev plan for the Visor repo against a fixed rubric (coverage, checkable criteria, no creep, placement, CLAUDE.md rules, honest size, assumptions, stop list). Use in step 2 of /visor-dev. Sees only the request, the plan, CLAUDE.md and docs/architecture.md. Read-only. ([tailamphat/visor](https://github.com/tailamphat/visor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visor-approver.md) · ★3)
- **visor-builder**: Runs ONE /visor-pm work item through the /visor-dev pipeline in managed mode, inside its own git worktree. Writes the item plan and stops; builds test first only when told "approved, build"; fixes gate findings, proves on the debug socket when it holds the screen lock, commits on its branch and returns a short Done report. Never spawns agents, never pushes. ([tailamphat/visor](https://github.com/tailamphat/visor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visor-builder.md) · ★3)
- **visor-perf**: Measures the CPU and memory cost of a Visor change with scripts/perf.sh (before vs after, optional sample of hot paths) and reads the diff for idle-cost risks. Use in the /visor-dev gate step. Never launches, quits or kills apps. Returns at most 8 findings. ([tailamphat/visor](https://github.com/tailamphat/visor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visor-perf.md) · ★3)
- **visor-reviewer**: Reviews a Visor diff for bugs and refactors (threading, state publishing, balanced notch, shared helpers, logic placement, missing tests). Use in the /visor-dev gate step. Gets only the diff, file list and relevant CLAUDE.md rules. Returns at most 8 findings with file:line and a failure scenario. Read-only. ([tailamphat/visor](https://github.com/tailamphat/visor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visor-reviewer.md) · ★3)

- **visual-critic-opus-5-5**: The visual/UX quality critic pinned to claude-opus-5-5, spawned beside the Fable visual critic on look-heavy work (rendering, terrain, water, any rendered frame; the review rules say when editor UI gets one critic) so two independent critics judge the same evidence set; same contract as visual-critic (ACCEPT or ITERATE with numbered findings, REJECT binding), reads only the captured evidence. ([Game-Crafters-Guild/OpenEngine](https://github.com/Game-Crafters-Guild/OpenEngine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visual-critic-opus-5-5.md) · ★300)
- **visual-critic-opus-5-5**: The visual/UX quality critic pinned to claude-opus-5-5, spawned beside the Fable visual critic on visual work (rendering, terrain, water, editor UI) so two independent critics judge the same evidence set; same contract as visual-critic (ACCEPT or ITERATE with numbered findings, REJECT binding), reads only the captured evidence. ([luciaengine/lucia-engine](https://github.com/luciaengine/lucia-engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/visual-critic-opus-5-5.md) · ★4)
- **visual-debug-iterator**: Iterate Phase 7 visual-fix cycles in a separate context with vision-free hard rule (no PNG/JPG/WebP reads). Invoked after section-compare.sh or tree-diff.sh reports FAIL. Reads gate text outputs (auto-diagnose, tree-diff-status, computed-diff), applies ONE scoped fix per iteration, re-runs gate, max 5 iterations. Reads the operational contract from skills/ui-reverse-engineering/iteration-discipline.md. Bailout cases (asset 404 / hydration / missing install / contract conflict) return immediately for pipeline-level intervention. Never use for greenfield generation. ([voidmatcha/ui-clone-skills](https://github.com/voidmatcha/ui-clone-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugin/agents/visual-debug-iterator.md) · ★17)
